  1425	def clone_released_code():
  1426	    """Clone the released CT-CLIP code and pin it to the contract commit."""
  1427	    import subprocess
  1428	    ctclip_dir = PROBE_DIR / "vendor" / "CT-CLIP"
  1429	    if not ctclip_dir.exists():
  1430	        ctclip_dir.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
  1431	        # Full clone (not depth-1): the pinned commit must be reachable even
  1432	        # if the upstream default branch has moved past it.
  1433	        r = subprocess.run(["git", "clone", CTCLIP_GIT_URL, str(ctclip_dir)],
  1434	                           capture_output=True, text=True)
  1435	        if r.returncode != 0:
  1436	            fail(3, f"git clone of released code failed: {r.stderr}")
  1437	    r = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(ctclip_dir), "checkout",
  1438	                        CODE_COMMIT], capture_output=True, text=True)
  1439	    if r.returncode != 0:
  1440	        fail(3, f"cannot checkout pinned commit {CODE_COMMIT}: {r.stderr}")
  1441	    head = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(ctclip_dir), "rev-parse", "HEAD"],
  1442	                          capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
  1443	    if head != CODE_COMMIT:
  1444	        fail(4, f"released code HEAD {head} != pinned {CODE_COMMIT}")
  1445	    dirty = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(ctclip_dir), "status",
  1446	                            "--porcelain"],
  1447	                           capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
  1448	    if dirty:
  1449	        # The released code must run UNMODIFIED; a dirty clone invalidates
  1450	        # the compatibility claim.
  1451	        fail(5, f"released-code clone has local modifications:\n{dirty}")
  1452	    log(f"  released code pinned at commit {head}")
  1453	    # The released packages live one directory below their project roots.
  1454	    for project in (ctclip_dir / "transformer_maskgit", ctclip_dir / "CT_CLIP"):
  1455	        if not (project / "setup.py").is_file():
  1456	            fail(11, f"released package root missing setup.py: {project}")
  1457	        sys.path.insert(0, str(project))
  1458	    sys.path.insert(0, str(ctclip_dir / "scripts"))
  1459	    return ctclip_dir
  1460	
  1461	
  1462	def strip_position_id_buffer_keys(state):
  1463	    """Remove *.embeddings.position_ids keys from a state dict, in place.
  1464	
  1465	    Returns the sorted removed-key list. The CALLER enforces the r6 contract
  1466	    semantics: EXACTLY ONE removed key is tolerable; zero or several mean the
  1467	    checkpoint is not the understood <=4.30-era artifact -> exit 5.
  1468	    """
  1469	    removed = sorted(k for k in state if POSITION_ID_BUFFER_PATTERN.match(k))
  1470	    for key in removed:
  1471	        del state[key]
  1472	    return removed
  1473	
  1474	
  1475	def load_released_model(checkpoint_path, provenance, out_dir):
  1476	    """Load the frozen checkpoint under the released code, r6-tolerantly.
  1477	
  1478	    Mirrors the released scripts/ct_lipro_inference.py construction exactly;
  1479	    any deviation needed to make this work is, by contract, an invalidating
  1480	    failure -- fix the DRIVER to match the released code, never the reverse.
  1481	    Returns (model, tokenizer, device).
  1482	    """
  1483	    import torch
  1484	    device = torch.device("cuda")
  1485	    try:
  1486	        from transformer_maskgit import CTViT
  1487	        from ct_clip import CTCLIP
  1488	        from ct_lipro_inference import ImageLatentsClassifier
  1489	        from transformers import BertTokenizer, BertModel
  1490	
  1491	        try:
  1492	            tokenizer = BertTokenizer.from_pretrained(TOKENIZER_NAME,
  1493	                                                      do_lower_case=True)
  1494	            text_encoder = BertModel.from_pretrained(TOKENIZER_NAME)
  1495	        except Exception as e:
  1496	            fail(3, f"tokenizer/text-encoder access failed: {e!r}")
  1497	        image_encoder = CTViT(
  1498	            dim=512, codebook_size=8192, image_size=480, patch_size=20,
  1499	            temporal_patch_size=10, spatial_depth=4, temporal_depth=4,
  1500	            dim_head=32, heads=8,
  1501	        )
  1502	        clip = CTCLIP(
  1503	            image_encoder=image_encoder, text_encoder=text_encoder,
  1504	            dim_image=294912, dim_text=768, dim_latent=512,
  1505	            extra_latent_projection=False, use_mlm=False,
  1506	            downsample_image_embeds=False, use_all_token_embeds=False,
  1507	        )
  1508	        model = ImageLatentsClassifier(clip, 512, num_classes=18)
  1509	        # weights_only=False matches the released loader's historical
  1510	        # full-checkpoint behavior explicitly (PyTorch 2.6 default change).
  1511	        state = torch.load(str(checkpoint_path), map_location="cpu",
  1512	                           weights_only=False)
  1513	        removed_keys = strip_position_id_buffer_keys(state)
  1514	        if len(removed_keys) != 1:
  1515	            fail(5, f"expected exactly one *.embeddings.position_ids buffer "
  1516	                    f"key, found {len(removed_keys)}: {removed_keys}; the "
  1517	                    f"checkpoint is not the understood <=4.30-era artifact")
  1518	        log(f"  r6 enumerated buffer strip: removed {removed_keys[0]!r}")
  1519	        provenance["state_dict_keys_removed_before_load"] = {
  1520	            "pattern": POSITION_ID_BUFFER_PATTERN.pattern,
  1521	            "removed_keys": removed_keys,
  1522	            "reason": ("transformers 4.31.0 made BERT "
  1523	                       "embeddings.position_ids a non-persistent buffer; the "
  1524	                       "<=4.30-era checkpoint carries it (r6 ledger, "
  1525	                       "2026-08-12)"),
  1526	        }
  1527	        write_json(out_dir / "provenance.json", provenance)
  1528	        # strict=True: any OTHER unexpected or missing key must fail here.
  1529	        model.load_state_dict(state, strict=True)
  1530	        model = model.cuda().eval()
  1531	    except SystemExit:
  1532	        raise
  1533	    except (ImportError, ModuleNotFoundError) as e:
  1534	        fail(11, f"released model dependency/import missing: {e!r}")
  1535	    except Exception as e:
  1536	        fail(5, f"released checkpoint/code failed to load unchanged: {e!r}")
  1537	    log("  checkpoint loaded: strict modulo the one enumerated, "
  1538	        "provenance-logged position_ids buffer key (r6)")
  1539	    return model, tokenizer, device
  1540

  1542	def build_inference_dataset(staging_root, tables):
  1543	    """Released preprocessing via the released dataset class.
  1544	
  1545	    staging_root must contain ONLY the volumes to score, laid out as
  1546	    <staging_root>/valid_P/valid_P_S/<name>.nii.gz. Returns (dataset,
  1547	    name -> index map).
  1548	    """
  1549	    try:
  1550	        from data_inference_nii import CTReportDatasetinfer
  1551	        dataset = CTReportDatasetinfer(
  1552	            data_folder=str(staging_root),
  1553	            reports_file=str(tables["validation_reports.csv"]),
  1554	            meta_file=str(tables["validation_metadata.csv"]),
  1555	            labels=str(tables["valid_predicted_labels.csv"]))
  1556	        index_by_name = {}
  1557	        for idx in range(len(dataset)):
  1558	            _video, _text, _onehot, acc = dataset[idx]
  1559	            index_by_name[str(acc)] = idx
  1560	    except SystemExit:
  1561	        raise
  1562	    except (ImportError, ModuleNotFoundError) as e:
  1563	        fail(11, f"released preprocessing import missing: {e!r}")
  1564	    except Exception as e:
  1565	        fail(9, f"released preprocessing failed on staged volumes: {e!r}")
  1566	    return dataset, index_by_name

  1569	def dataset_index_for(index_by_name, volume_name):
  1570	    """Map a VolumeName to its released-dataset item, uniquely."""
  1571	    stem = volume_name.replace(".nii.gz", "")
  1572	    matches = [k for k in index_by_name if stem in k]
  1573	    if len(matches) != 1:
  1574	        fail(9, f"staged volume {volume_name} not found uniquely in released "
  1575	                f"dataset items: {sorted(index_by_name)}")
  1576	    return index_by_name[matches[0]]

  1579	def stage_volume(local_path, staging_root, volume_name):
  1580	    """Hard-link one downloaded volume into the released dataset layout."""
  1581	    patient, scan, _ = parse_volume_name(volume_name)
  1582	    dest = staging_root / f"valid_{patient}" / f"valid_{patient}_{scan}"
  1583	    dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1584	    link = dest / volume_name
  1585	    if not link.exists():
  1586	        os.link(local_path, link)
  1587	    return link

  1590	def make_scorer(model, tokenizer, device):
  1591	    """Return score(dataset, idx) -> ([18 float64 probabilities], metrics).
  1592	
  1593	    Empty-string text tokens, exactly as the released inference does; batch
  1594	    size 1 always (contract configuration).
  1595	    """
  1596	    import torch
  1597	    text_tokens = tokenizer([""], return_tensors="pt", padding="max_length",
  1598	                            truncation=True, max_length=512).to("cuda")
  1599	
  1600	    def score(dataset, idx):
  1601	        video, _text, _onehot, _acc = dataset[idx]
  1602	        video = video.unsqueeze(0).cuda()   # batch size 1, per contract
  1603	        torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
  1604	        start = time.monotonic()
  1605	        with torch.no_grad():
  1606	            # Released ImageLatentsClassifier call signature: False asks for
  1607	            # classifier logits rather than the 512-dim latents.
  1608	            logits = model(False, text_tokens, video, device=device)
  1609	        scores = torch.sigmoid(logits).flatten().double().cpu().tolist()
  1610	        return scores, {
  1611	            "seconds": time.monotonic() - start,
  1612	            "peak_gpu_memory_gb": torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1e9,
  1613	        }
  1614	
  1615	    return score
  1616

  1619	# PHASE M -- METADATA-ONLY MANIFEST FREEZE. This is everything the phase-1
  1620	# approval authorizes: fetch and hash-verify the three pinned tables,

  1811	def chunk_dir_for(out_dir, chunk_no):
  1812	    return out_dir / "chunks" / f"chunk_{chunk_no:02d}"
  1813	
  1814	
  1815	def chunk_is_complete(chunk_dir):
  1816	    """A chunk counts as complete only when environment.json -- written LAST
  1817	    -- says so and its sibling artifacts exist. Anything else is an
  1818	    interrupted chunk and is redone in full (contract chunk_atomicity)."""
  1819	    env_path = chunk_dir / "environment.json"
  1820	    if not env_path.exists():
  1821	        return False
  1822	    try:
  1823	        env = json.loads(env_path.read_text())
  1824	    except json.JSONDecodeError:
  1825	        return False
  1826	    return (env.get("chunk_complete") is True
  1827	            and (chunk_dir / "chunk_manifest.csv").exists()
  1828	            and (chunk_dir / "per_sample.csv").exists())
  1829	
  1830	
  1831	def spot_check_pair_ids(pairs):
  1832	    """The preregistered spot-check subset: the FIRST pair of each stratum in
  1833	    manifest order (contract determinism.spot_check_subset)."""
  1834	    first = {}
  1835	    for pair in pairs:
  1836	        if pair["stratum"] not in first:
  1837	            first[pair["stratum"]] = pair["pair_id"]
  1838	    return set(first.values())
  1839	
  1840	
  1841	class DownloadLedger:
  1842	    """Persistent download accounting against the R8 volume caps.
  1843	
  1844	    Categories:
  1845	      scientific_first_time -- first chunk-context download of a distinct
  1846	          manifest volume; capped by unique_volume_cap.
  1847	      scientific_shared_reuse -- the same manifest volume downloaded again by
  1848	          a DIFFERENT chunk (volumes shared across strata); reported, not
  1849	          capped (it is neither a distinct volume nor a QA retry).
  1850	      qa_retry -- chunk-redo downloads and anchor (re)downloads; capped by
  1851	          ceil(0.20 x unique_volume_cap) (contract qa_retry_download_allowance).
  1852	    Reaching either cap stops the run as budget exhaustion (exit 9).
  1853	    """
  1854	
  1855	    def __init__(self, path, unique_volume_cap):
  1856	        self.path = path
  1857	        self.unique_cap = unique_volume_cap
  1858	        self.qa_cap = math.ceil(QA_RETRY_FRACTION * unique_volume_cap)
  1859	        rows = read_csv_rows(path)
  1860	        self.first_time_volumes = {
  1861	            r["volume_name"] for r in rows
  1862	            if r["category"] == "scientific_first_time"}
  1863	        self.chunk_seen_volumes = {
  1864	            r["volume_name"] for r in rows
  1865	            if r["context"].startswith("chunk")}
  1866	        self.qa_count = sum(1 for r in rows if r["category"] == "qa_retry")
  1867	
  1868	    def categorize(self, context, volume_name, redo):
  1869	        if context == "anchor" or redo:
  1870	            return "qa_retry"
  1871	        if volume_name in self.chunk_seen_volumes:
  1872	            return "scientific_shared_reuse"
  1873	        return "scientific_first_time"
  1874	
  1875	    def check_budget_before(self, category):
  1876	        """Refuse BEFORE the download that would cross a cap (contract R8:
  1877	        continuing past a cap is invalidating)."""
  1878	        if (category == "scientific_first_time"
  1879	                and len(self.first_time_volumes) + 1 > self.unique_cap):
  1880	            fail(9, f"unique-volume cap {self.unique_cap} would be exceeded; "
  1881	                    f"stopping as budget exhaustion (not a scientific result)")
  1882	        if category == "qa_retry" and self.qa_count + 1 > self.qa_cap:
  1883	            fail(9, f"QA/retry download allowance {self.qa_cap} would be "
  1884	                    f"exceeded; stopping as budget exhaustion "
  1885	                    f"(not a scientific result)")
  1886	
  1887	    def record(self, session_id, context, volume_name, sha256, size_bytes,
  1888	               category):
  1889	        if category == "scientific_first_time":
  1890	            self.first_time_volumes.add(volume_name)
  1891	        if context.startswith("chunk"):
  1892	            self.chunk_seen_volumes.add(volume_name)
  1893	        if category == "qa_retry":
  1894	            self.qa_count += 1
  1895	        append_csv_rows(self.path, [{
  1896	            "timestamp_utc": utc_now(), "session_id": session_id,
  1897	            "context": context, "volume_name": volume_name,
  1898	            "sha256": sha256, "size_bytes": size_bytes,
  1899	            "category": category,
  1900	        }], ACCOUNTING_COLUMNS)
  1901	
  1902	    def totals(self):
  1903	        return {
  1904	            "scientific_first_time": len(self.first_time_volumes),
  1905	            "unique_volume_cap": self.unique_cap,
  1906	            "qa_retry": self.qa_count,
  1907	            "qa_retry_allowance": self.qa_cap,
  1908	        }
  1909	
  1910	
  1911	def run_anchor_protocol(out_dir, work_dir, session_id, tables, scorer,
  1912	                        reference, ledger):
  1913	    """The per-session anchor drift detector (contract execution_model.anchor).
  1914	
  1915	    Runs A, B, A-again on the v1 load-probe pair BEFORE any chunk work.
  1916	    Within-session: the A repeat must be bit-identical (exit 8). Cross-
  1917	    session: per-head probabilities of A and B must sit within 1.0e-4 of the
  1918	    pinned v1 reference (exit 13). All anchor executions are excluded from
  1919	    every scientific statistic and logged to anchor_log.csv.
  1920	    """
  1921	    log("anchor protocol: session drift detector (A, B, A-repeat)")
  1922	    cache_dir = out_dir.parent / "anchor_cache_004"
  1923	    cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1924	    local = {}
  1925	    cached_flags = {}
  1926	    for name in (ANCHOR_VOLUME_A, ANCHOR_VOLUME_B):
  1927	        expected_sha = ANCHOR_INPUT_SHA256[name]
  1928	        cache_path = cache_dir / name
  1929	        # SHA-256 verified before EVERY use, cached or fresh (contract).
  1930	        if cache_path.exists() and sha256_file(cache_path) == expected_sha:
  1931	            cached_flags[name] = True
  1932	        else:
  1933	            ledger.check_budget_before("qa_retry")
  1934	            downloaded = hf_download(volume_repo_path(name),
  1935	                                     work_dir / "anchor_dl")
  1936	            actual = sha256_file(downloaded)
  1937	            if actual != expected_sha:
  1938	                fail(4, f"anchor volume {name} hash {actual} != pinned "
  1939	                        f"{expected_sha}")
  1940	            shutil.copyfile(downloaded, cache_path)
  1941	            ledger.record(session_id, "anchor", name, actual,
  1942	                          cache_path.stat().st_size, "qa_retry")
  1943	            cached_flags[name] = False
  1944	        local[name] = cache_path
  1945	        log(f"  anchor {name}: "
  1946	            f"{'cache hit' if cached_flags[name] else 'downloaded'}, "
  1947	            f"hash verified")
  1948	
  1949	    staging = work_dir / "anchor_stage"
  1950	    if staging.exists():
  1951	        shutil.rmtree(staging)
  1952	    staging.mkdir(parents=True)
  1953	    for name in (ANCHOR_VOLUME_A, ANCHOR_VOLUME_B):
  1954	        stage_volume(local[name], staging, name)
  1955	    dataset, index_by_name = build_inference_dataset(staging, tables)
  1956	    idx_a = dataset_index_for(index_by_name, ANCHOR_VOLUME_A)
  1957	    idx_b = dataset_index_for(index_by_name, ANCHOR_VOLUME_B)
  1958	
  1959	    executions = []
  1960	    for exec_id, idx, name in [("anchor_A", idx_a, ANCHOR_VOLUME_A),
  1961	                               ("anchor_B", idx_b, ANCHOR_VOLUME_B),
  1962	                               ("anchor_A_repeat", idx_a, ANCHOR_VOLUME_A)]:
  1963	        scores, _metrics = scorer(dataset, idx)
  1964	        check_18_finite(scores, exec_id)
  1965	        executions.append((exec_id, name, scores))
  1966	        log(f"  {exec_id}: 18 finite scores")
  1967	
  1968	    a_first = executions[0][2]
  1969	    a_repeat = executions[2][2]
  1970	    bit_identical = scores_hex(a_first) == scores_hex(a_repeat)
  1971	    dev_a = max_abs_deviation(a_first, reference["exec1_A"])
  1972	    dev_b = max_abs_deviation(executions[1][2], reference["exec2_B"])
  1973	
  1974	    rows = []
  1975	    for exec_id, name, scores in executions:
  1976	        dev = {"anchor_A": dev_a, "anchor_B": dev_b}.get(exec_id, "")
  1977	        rows.append({
  1978	            "session_id": session_id,
  1979	            "timestamp_utc": utc_now(),
  1980	            "execution": exec_id,
  1981	            "volume_name": name,
  1982	            "cached": str(cached_flags[name]).lower(),
  1983	            "within_session_bit_identical":
  1984	                str(bit_identical).lower() if exec_id == "anchor_A_repeat"
  1985	                else "",
  1986	            "max_abs_dev_vs_v1": repr(dev) if dev != "" else "",
  1987	            "tolerance": repr(ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE),
  1988	            "in_tolerance":
  1989	                str(bool(dev <= ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE)).lower()
  1990	                if dev != "" else "",
  1991	            "scores_hex": "|".join(scores_hex(scores)),
  1992	        })
  1993	    append_csv_rows(out_dir / "anchor" / "anchor_log.csv", rows,
  1994	                    ANCHOR_LOG_COLUMNS)
  1995	    shutil.rmtree(staging)
  1996	
  1997	    if not bit_identical:
  1998	        fail(8, "anchor A-repeat was NOT bit-identical within this session; "
  1999	                "invalidating determinism failure")
  2000	    if dev_a > ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE or \
  2001	            dev_b > ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE:
  2002	        fail(13, f"anchor cross-session drift beyond the preregistered "
  2003	                 f"{ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE} tolerance "
  2004	                 f"(dev_A={dev_a!r}, dev_B={dev_b!r}); this session's chunks "
  2005	                 f"do not count and are redone after diagnosis")
  2006	    log(f"  anchor in tolerance (dev_A={dev_a:.2e}, dev_B={dev_b:.2e}, "
  2007	        f"tol={ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE}); A-repeat bit-identical")
  2008	
  2009	
  2010	def run_chunk(out_dir, work_dir, session_id, chunk_no, chunk_pairs, tables,
  2011	              scorer, ledger, spot_ids, metadata_by_volume, env_info):
  2012	    """Execute one whole chunk in this session (contract R7 chunk_atomicity).
  2013	
  2014	    download -> hash-record (persisted BEFORE inference) -> preprocess ->
  2015	    infer both members of every pair -> spot-check re-runs -> delete image
  2016	    files -> environment.json written LAST as the completion marker.
  2017	    """
  2018	    chunk_dir = chunk_dir_for(out_dir, chunk_no)
  2019	    redo = chunk_dir.exists() and any(chunk_dir.iterdir())
  2020	    if redo:
  2021	        log(f"chunk {chunk_no:02d}: interrupted remains found; redoing IN "
  2022	            f"FULL (contract chunk_atomicity)")
  2023	        shutil.rmtree(chunk_dir)
  2024	    chunk_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  2025	    chunk_start = time.monotonic()
  2026	    log(f"chunk {chunk_no:02d}: {len(chunk_pairs)} pairs "
  2027	        f"({chunk_pairs[0]['pair_id']}..{chunk_pairs[-1]['pair_id']})"
  2028	        f"{' [redo]' if redo else ''}")
  2029	
  2030	    dl_dir = work_dir / f"chunk_{chunk_no:02d}_dl"
  2031	    staging = work_dir / f"chunk_{chunk_no:02d}_stage"
  2032	    for d in (dl_dir, staging):
  2033	        if d.exists():
  2034	            shutil.rmtree(d)
  2035	        d.mkdir(parents=True)
  2036	
  2037	    # -- download and hash EVERY input, then persist chunk_manifest.csv
  2038	    #    BEFORE any inference (contract: inference on a volume before its
  2039	    #    hash is recorded is invalidating).
  2040	    manifest_rows = []
  2041	    local_paths = {}
  2042	    n_done = 0
  2043	    for pair in chunk_pairs:
  2044	        for role in ("softer", "sharper"):
  2045	            name = pair[f"volume_name_{role}"]
  2046	            assert_validation_only(name)
  2047	            category = ledger.categorize(f"chunk_{chunk_no:02d}", name, redo)
  2048	            ledger.check_budget_before(category)
  2049	            path = hf_download(volume_repo_path(name), dl_dir)
  2050	            sha = sha256_file(path)
  2051	            size = path.stat().st_size
  2052	            ledger.record(session_id, f"chunk_{chunk_no:02d}", name, sha,
  2053	                          size, category)
  2054	            local_paths[name] = path
  2055	            manifest_rows.append({
  2056	                "chunk": f"chunk_{chunk_no:02d}", "pair_id": pair["pair_id"],
  2057	                "role": role, "volume_name": name, "sha256": sha,
  2058	                "size_bytes": size, "session_id": session_id,
  2059	                "downloaded_utc": utc_now(),
  2060	            })
  2061	            n_done += 1
  2062	            log(f"  [{n_done}/{2 * len(chunk_pairs)}] downloaded+hashed "
  2063	                f"{name} ({size / 1e6:.0f} MB, {category})")
  2064	    write_csv(chunk_dir / "chunk_manifest.csv", manifest_rows,
  2065	              CHUNK_MANIFEST_COLUMNS)
  2066	    log(f"  chunk_manifest.csv persisted ({len(manifest_rows)} volumes) "
  2067	        f"BEFORE inference")
  2068	
  2069	    # -- released preprocessing over exactly this chunk's volumes.
  2070	    for name, path in local_paths.items():
  2071	        stage_volume(path, staging, name)
  2072	    dataset, index_by_name = build_inference_dataset(staging, tables)
  2073	
  2074	    # -- inference: both members of every pair, in manifest order, batch 1.
  2075	    sample_rows = []
  2076	    first_run_hex = {}   # (pair_id, role) -> score_hex list
  2077	    inference_seconds = 0.0
  2078	    peak_gb = 0.0
  2079	    try:
  2080	        for i, pair in enumerate(chunk_pairs, start=1):
  2081	            for role in ("softer", "sharper"):
  2082	                name = pair[f"volume_name_{role}"]
  2083	                exec_id = f"{pair['pair_id']}_{role}"
  2084	                idx = dataset_index_for(index_by_name, name)
  2085	                scores, metrics = scorer(dataset, idx)
  2086	                check_18_finite(scores, exec_id)
  2087	                inference_seconds += metrics["seconds"]
  2088	                peak_gb = max(peak_gb, metrics["peak_gpu_memory_gb"])
  2089	                hexes = scores_hex(scores)
  2090	                first_run_hex[(pair["pair_id"], role)] = hexes
  2091	                for h in range(18):
  2092	                    sample_rows.append({
  2093	                        "session_id": session_id,
  2094	                        "chunk": f"chunk_{chunk_no:02d}",
  2095	                        "execution": exec_id,
  2096	                        "pair_id": pair["pair_id"],
  2097	                        "role": role,
  2098	                        "volume_name": name,
  2099	                        "stratum": pair["stratum"],
  2100	                        "head_index": h,
  2101	                        "head_name": EXPECTED_PATHOLOGIES[h],
  2102	                        "score": repr(float(scores[h])),
  2103	                        "score_hex": hexes[h],
  2104	                    })
  2105	            log(f"  [{i}/{len(chunk_pairs)}] scored pair "
  2106	                f"{pair['pair_id']} ({pair['stratum']})")
  2107	
  2108	        # -- preregistered within-session spot-check re-runs (contract R9):
  2109	        #    first pair of each stratum; both members; bit-identical or
  2110	        #    invalidating. Re-run rows are excluded from scientific stats.
  2111	        for pair in chunk_pairs:
  2112	            if pair["pair_id"] not in spot_ids:
  2113	                continue
  2114	            log(f"  spot-check re-run for {pair['pair_id']} "
  2115	                f"(preregistered subset)")
  2116	            for role in ("softer", "sharper"):
  2117	                name = pair[f"volume_name_{role}"]
  2118	                exec_id = f"{pair['pair_id']}_{role}_spotcheck"
  2119	                idx = dataset_index_for(index_by_name, name)
  2120	                scores, _metrics = scorer(dataset, idx)
  2121	                check_18_finite(scores, exec_id)
  2122	                hexes = scores_hex(scores)
  2123	                if hexes != first_run_hex[(pair["pair_id"], role)]:
  2124	                    fail(8, f"spot-check re-run {exec_id} NOT bit-identical "
  2125	                            f"to its first run in this session; invalidating "
  2126	                            f"determinism failure")
  2127	                for h in range(18):
  2128	                    sample_rows.append({
  2129	                        "session_id": session_id,
  2130	                        "chunk": f"chunk_{chunk_no:02d}",
  2131	                        "execution": exec_id,
  2132	                        "pair_id": pair["pair_id"],
  2133	                        "role": role,
  2134	                        "volume_name": name,
  2135	                        "stratum": pair["stratum"],
  2136	                        "head_index": h,
  2137	                        "head_name": EXPECTED_PATHOLOGIES[h],
  2138	                        "score": repr(float(scores[h])),
  2139	                        "score_hex": hexes[h],
  2140	                    })
  2141	            log(f"  spot-check {pair['pair_id']}: bit-identical")
  2142	    except SystemExit:
  2143	        raise
  2144	    except Exception as e:
  2145	        import torch
  2146	        if isinstance(e, torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError):
  2147	            fail(9, f"batch-size-1 inference exceeded GPU memory: {e}")
  2148	        fail(9, f"inference crashed: {e!r}")
  2149	
  2150	    write_csv(chunk_dir / "per_sample.csv", sample_rows,
  2151	              CHUNK_PER_SAMPLE_COLUMNS)
  2152	
  2153	    # -- delete image files (contract R7: download -> ... -> delete).
  2154	    shutil.rmtree(dl_dir)
  2155	    shutil.rmtree(staging)
  2156	    log(f"  chunk {chunk_no:02d}: image files deleted")
  2157	
  2158	    # -- environment.json LAST: it is the chunk-completion marker.
  2159	    write_json(chunk_dir / "environment.json", {
  2160	        "chunk": f"chunk_{chunk_no:02d}",
  2161	        "chunk_complete": True,
  2162	        "session_id": session_id,
  2163	        "redo": redo,
  2164	        "gpu": env_info.get("gpu"),
  2165	        "cuda": env_info.get("cuda"),
  2166	        "torch": env_info.get("torch"),
  2167	        "python": env_info.get("python"),
  2168	        "transformers": env_info.get("transformers"),
  2169	        "tokenizers": env_info.get("tokenizers"),
  2170	        "numpy": env_info.get("numpy"),
  2171	        "wall_seconds": time.monotonic() - chunk_start,
  2172	        "inference_seconds": inference_seconds,
  2173	        "peak_gpu_memory_gb": peak_gb,
  2174	        "timestamp_utc": utc_now(),
  2175	    })
  2176	    log(f"  chunk {chunk_no:02d} COMPLETE "
  2177	        f"({time.monotonic() - chunk_start:.0f}s wall, "
  2178	        f"{inference_seconds:.0f}s inference, peak {peak_gb:.2f} GB)")
  2179	
  2180	
  2181	def collect_chunk_scores(out_dir, pairs, chunks):
  2182	    """Assemble per-pair scores from the persisted chunk artifacts.
  2183	
  2184	    Uses score_hex (exact float64 round-trip). Spot-check re-run rows are
  2185	    excluded from the scientific set. Every manifest pair must have both
  2310	    }
  2311	
  2312	
  2313	def run_phase_b(out_dir, gate_info):
  2314	    log("PHASE B: bulk 425-pair floor study (session start)")
  2315	    env_info = capture_environment(out_dir, "B", need_gpu=True)
  2316	    work_dir = PROBE_DIR / "work"
  2317	    work_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
  2318	
  2319	    session_id = (time.strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ", time.gmtime())
  2320	                  + "-" + os.urandom(4).hex())
  2321	    # Session cap (contract R8): sessions are counted by their anchor-log
  2322	    # entries, because the anchor protocol is mandatory at every session
  2323	    # start; a 31st session must not begin.
  2324	    prior_sessions = {r["session_id"] for r in read_csv_rows(
  2325	        out_dir / "anchor" / "anchor_log.csv")}
  2326	    if len(prior_sessions) >= SESSION_CAP:
  2327	        fail(9, f"session cap {SESSION_CAP} reached "
  2328	                f"({len(prior_sessions)} prior sessions); stopping as "
  2329	                f"budget exhaustion (not a scientific result)")
  2330	    log(f"  session {session_id} "
  2331	        f"({len(prior_sessions)} prior sessions, cap {SESSION_CAP})")
  2332	
  2333	    write_resolved_config(out_dir, "B", extra={"session_id": session_id})
  2334	
  2335	    # -- provenance: tables, manifest regeneration, checkpoint, code, v1 ref.
  2336	    tables = obtain_pinned_tables(work_dir)
  2337	    with open(tables["validation_metadata.csv"], newline="") as f:
  2338	        metadata_rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
  2339	    metadata_by_volume = {r["VolumeName"]: r for r in metadata_rows}
  2340	
  2341	    pairs, audit = build_pair_manifest(
  2342	        metadata_rows, FROZEN_STRATUM_COUNTS,
  2343	        (ANCHOR_VOLUME_A, ANCHOR_VOLUME_B))
  2344	    manifest_dir = out_dir / "manifest"
  2345	    manifest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  2346	    write_json(manifest_dir / "selection_audit.json", audit)
  2347	    if not audit["counts_match"]:
  2348	        report_selection_shortfall(audit, FROZEN_STRATUM_COUNTS,
  2349	                                   manifest_dir / "selection_audit.json")
  2350	        fail(7, f"phase-B manifest regeneration counts "
  2351	                f"{audit['selected_counts']} differ from frozen "
  2352	                f"{FROZEN_STRATUM_COUNTS}")
  2353	    manifest_bytes = manifest_csv_bytes(pairs)
  2354	    manifest_sha = sha256_bytes(manifest_bytes)
  2355	    if manifest_sha != gate_info["pair_manifest_sha256"]:
  2356	        fail(7, f"regenerated manifest sha256 {manifest_sha} != contract-"
  2357	                f"recorded {gate_info['pair_manifest_sha256']}; the analysis "
  2358	                f"population is the recorded manifest byte-for-byte, so stop")
  2359	    if audit["unique_volume_count"] != gate_info["unique_volume_count"]:
  2360	        fail(7, f"regenerated unique-volume count "
  2361	                f"{audit['unique_volume_count']} != contract-recorded "
  2362	                f"{gate_info['unique_volume_count']}")
  2363	    manifest_path = manifest_dir / "pair_manifest.csv"
  2364	    if manifest_path.exists() and \
  2365	            manifest_path.read_bytes() != manifest_bytes:
  2366	        fail(7, "on-disk pair_manifest.csv differs from the regenerated "
  2367	                "frozen manifest; refusing to proceed over a divergent copy")
  2368	    manifest_path.write_bytes(manifest_bytes)
  2369	    log(f"  manifest verified against contract hash "
  2370	        f"({manifest_sha[:12]}..., {len(pairs)} pairs, "
  2371	        f"{audit['unique_volume_count']} unique volumes)")
  2372	
  2373	    chunks = chunk_plan(pairs, CHUNK_SIZE_PAIRS)
  2374	    if len(chunks) != CHUNK_COUNT or len(pairs) != TOTAL_PAIRS:
  2375	        fail(12, f"chunk plan {len(chunks)} x {CHUNK_SIZE_PAIRS} does not "
  2376	                 f"cover {len(pairs)} pairs as 17 x 25; harness fault")
  2377	    spot_ids = spot_check_pair_ids(pairs)
  2378	    log(f"  preregistered spot-check pairs: {sorted(spot_ids)}")
  2379	
  2380	    checkpoint_path = hf_download(CHECKPOINT_REPO_PATH, work_dir)
  2381	    checkpoint_sha = sha256_file(checkpoint_path)
  2382	    if checkpoint_sha != CHECKPOINT_SHA256:
  2383	        fail(4, f"checkpoint hash {checkpoint_sha} != pinned "
  2384	                f"{CHECKPOINT_SHA256}")
  2385	    log("  checkpoint hash verified against the v1-frozen pin")
  2386	    clone_released_code()
  2387	    reference = load_v1_reference()
  2388	    log("  v1 anchor reference loaded (git blob verified)")
  2389	
  2390	    provenance = {
  2391	        "phase": "B",
  2392	        "session_id": session_id,
  2393	        "timestamp_utc": utc_now(),
  2394	        "contract_blob": gate_info["contract_blob"],
  2395	        "approval_marker_blob": gate_info["marker_blob"],
  2396	        "hf_dataset_repo": HF_DATASET_REPO,
  2397	        "hf_revision": HF_REVISION,
  2398	        "tables_sha256_verified": TABLES_SHA256,
  2399	        "checkpoint": {"repo_path": CHECKPOINT_REPO_PATH,
  2400	                       "sha256": checkpoint_sha,
  2401	                       "attribution_note": (
  2402	                           "attribution limited to 'released v2 ClassFine "
  2403	                           "checkpoint' until paper-number correspondence "
  2404	                           "is checked (2026-08-11 pin 4)")},
  2405	        "code_commit": CODE_COMMIT,
  2406	        "pair_manifest_sha256": manifest_sha,
  2407	        "v1_anchor_reference_blob": V1_REFERENCE_GIT_BLOB,
  2408	    }
  2409	    write_json(out_dir / "provenance.json", provenance)
  2410	
  2411	    model, tokenizer, device = load_released_model(checkpoint_path,
  2412	                                                   provenance, out_dir)
  2413	    scorer = make_scorer(model, tokenizer, device)
  2414	
  2415	    ledger = DownloadLedger(out_dir / "scores" / "download_accounting.csv",
  2416	                            gate_info["unique_volume_count"])
  2417	    # Anchor protocol FIRST, at every session start, before any chunk work
  2418	    # (contract execution_model.anchor). Its failure modes exit here.
  2419	    run_anchor_protocol(out_dir, work_dir, session_id, tables, scorer,
  2420	                        reference, ledger)
  2421	
  2422	    # -- chunk loop: whole chunks only, in order, skipping completed ones.
  2423	    completed = [n for n, _ in chunks if chunk_is_complete(
  2424	        chunk_dir_for(out_dir, n))]
  2425	    log(f"  chunks already complete: {completed or 'none'}")
  2426	    for chunk_no, chunk_pairs in chunks:
  2427	        if chunk_is_complete(chunk_dir_for(out_dir, chunk_no)):
  2428	            continue
  2429	        run_chunk(out_dir, work_dir, session_id, chunk_no, chunk_pairs,
  2430	                  tables, scorer, ledger, spot_ids, metadata_by_volume,
  2431	                  env_info)
  2432	
  2433	    # -- all chunks complete: frozen analysis (tier 2 only if tier 1 ran).
  2434	    analysis_counts = run_analysis(out_dir, pairs, chunks, tables,
  2435	                                   metadata_by_volume)
  2436	
  2437	    summary = {
  2438	        "idea_id": IDEA_ID,
  2439	        "contract_version": CONTRACT_VERSION,
  2440	        "phase": "B",
  2441	        "mode": "real",
  2442	        "contract_satisfied": True,
  2443	        "session_id": session_id,
  2444	        "sessions_used": len(prior_sessions) + 1,
  2445	        "session_cap": SESSION_CAP,
  2446	        "pair_count": len(pairs),
  2447	        "pair_manifest_sha256": manifest_sha,
  2448	        "unique_volume_count": audit["unique_volume_count"],
  2449	        "download_accounting": ledger.totals(),
  2450	        "chunks_complete": CHUNK_COUNT,
  2451	        "spot_check_pairs": sorted(spot_ids),
  2452	        "anchor_pair_confirmatory_exclusion": (
  2453	            "Br40f|Br60f confirmatory statistics use 236 counted pairs "
  2454	            "(237 raw); the anchor pair's deltas are excluded everywhere"),
  2455	        "analysis": analysis_counts,
  2456	        "outputs": {
  2457	            "tier1": ["analysis/tier1_differences.csv",
  2458	                      "analysis/tier1_stats.csv",
  2459	                      "analysis/tier1_bootstrap.csv"],
  2460	            "tier2": ["analysis/tier2_auroc.csv",
  2461	                      "analysis/tier2_excluded_cells.csv"],
  2462	        },
  2463	        "interpretation": (
  2464	            "POSITIVE per the contract's positive_pattern: the frozen "
  2465	            "manifest was reproduced and hash-verified, every chunk "
  2466	            "completed with verified provenance, same-session pairing, "
  2467	            "bit-identical spot-checks, and in-tolerance anchors; all 425 "
  2468	            "pairs were scored and the frozen two-tier analysis ran. ANY "
  2469	            "resulting magnitude profile -- near-zero, large, or mixed "
  2470	            "across heads and strata -- is a valid descriptive outcome: the "
  2471	            "deliverable is the measured reconstruction-sensitivity "
  2472	            "baseline itself, for the released v2 ClassFine checkpoint on "
  2473	            "these CT-RATE kernel contrasts in a predominantly single-"
  2474	            "vendor (Siemens) cohort. Per the negative_pattern there is no "
  2475	            "failing magnitude: tightly-bounded-near-zero paired "
  2476	            "differences are the sensitivity-bounded descriptive null, and "
  2477	            "large paired differences are equally valid descriptively. No "
  2478	            "equivalence, robustness, accuracy, concept-validity, "
  2479	            "localization, or cross-vendor claim is made or implied; tier-2 "
  2480	            "numbers are benchmark discrimination against report-derived "
  2481	            "labels, never clinical accuracy, and carry no pass/fail "
  2482	            "semantics."),
  2483	    }
  2484	    write_json(out_dir / "summary.json", summary)
  2485	    print()
  2486	    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
  2487	    print()
  2488	    print("Plain-English read: the study is complete. The per-head,")
  2489	    print("per-stratum tables under analysis/ ARE the deliverable -- a")
  2490	    print("reconstruction-sensitivity baseline for this checkpoint on these")
  2491	    print("contrasts. Interpretation belongs to the interpret stage; no")
  2492	    print("magnitude here passes or fails anything.")
  2493	    return 0

  1430	        ctclip_dir.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
  1431	        # Full clone (not depth-1): the pinned commit must be reachable even
  1432	        # if the upstream default branch has moved past it.
  1433	        r = subprocess.run(["git", "clone", CTCLIP_GIT_URL, str(ctclip_dir)],
  1434	                           capture_output=True, text=True)
  1435	        if r.returncode != 0:
  1436	            fail(3, f"git clone of released code failed: {r.stderr}")
  1437	    r = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(ctclip_dir), "checkout",
  1438	                        CODE_COMMIT], capture_output=True, text=True)
  1439	    if r.returncode != 0:
  1440	        fail(3, f"cannot checkout pinned commit {CODE_COMMIT}: {r.stderr}")
  1441	    head = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(ctclip_dir), "rev-parse", "HEAD"],
  1442	                          capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
  1443	    if head != CODE_COMMIT:
  1444	        fail(4, f"released code HEAD {head} != pinned {CODE_COMMIT}")
  1445	    dirty = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(ctclip_dir), "status",
  1446	                            "--porcelain"],
  1447	                           capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
  1448	    if dirty:
  1449	        # The released code must run UNMODIFIED; a dirty clone invalidates
  1450	        # the compatibility claim.
  1451	        fail(5, f"released-code clone has local modifications:\n{dirty}")
  1452	    log(f"  released code pinned at commit {head}")
  1453	    # The released packages live one directory below their project roots.
  1454	    for project in (ctclip_dir / "transformer_maskgit", ctclip_dir / "CT_CLIP"):
  1455	        if not (project / "setup.py").is_file():
  1456	            fail(11, f"released package root missing setup.py: {project}")
  1457	        sys.path.insert(0, str(project))
  1458	    sys.path.insert(0, str(ctclip_dir / "scripts"))
  1459	    return ctclip_dir

  1462	def strip_position_id_buffer_keys(state):
  1463	    """Remove *.embeddings.position_ids keys from a state dict, in place.
  1464	
  1465	    Returns the sorted removed-key list. The CALLER enforces the r6 contract
  1466	    semantics: EXACTLY ONE removed key is tolerable; zero or several mean the
  1467	    checkpoint is not the understood <=4.30-era artifact -> exit 5.
  1468	    """
  1469	    removed = sorted(k for k in state if POSITION_ID_BUFFER_PATTERN.match(k))
  1470	    for key in removed:
  1471	        del state[key]
  1472	    return removed

  1475	def load_released_model(checkpoint_path, provenance, out_dir):
  1476	    """Load the frozen checkpoint under the released code, r6-tolerantly.
  1477	
  1478	    Mirrors the released scripts/ct_lipro_inference.py construction exactly;
  1479	    any deviation needed to make this work is, by contract, an invalidating
  1480	    failure -- fix the DRIVER to match the released code, never the reverse.
  1481	    Returns (model, tokenizer, device).
  1482	    """
  1483	    import torch
  1484	    device = torch.device("cuda")
  1485	    try:
  1486	        from transformer_maskgit import CTViT
  1487	        from ct_clip import CTCLIP
  1488	        from ct_lipro_inference import ImageLatentsClassifier
  1489	        from transformers import BertTokenizer, BertModel
  1490	
  1491	        try:
  1492	            tokenizer = BertTokenizer.from_pretrained(TOKENIZER_NAME,
  1493	                                                      do_lower_case=True)
  1494	            text_encoder = BertModel.from_pretrained(TOKENIZER_NAME)
  1495	        except Exception as e:
  1496	            fail(3, f"tokenizer/text-encoder access failed: {e!r}")
  1497	        image_encoder = CTViT(
  1498	            dim=512, codebook_size=8192, image_size=480, patch_size=20,
  1499	            temporal_patch_size=10, spatial_depth=4, temporal_depth=4,
  1500	            dim_head=32, heads=8,
  1501	        )
  1502	        clip = CTCLIP(
  1503	            image_encoder=image_encoder, text_encoder=text_encoder,
  1504	            dim_image=294912, dim_text=768, dim_latent=512,
  1505	            extra_latent_projection=False, use_mlm=False,
  1506	            downsample_image_embeds=False, use_all_token_embeds=False,
  1507	        )
  1508	        model = ImageLatentsClassifier(clip, 512, num_classes=18)
  1509	        # weights_only=False matches the released loader's historical
  1510	        # full-checkpoint behavior explicitly (PyTorch 2.6 default change).
  1511	        state = torch.load(str(checkpoint_path), map_location="cpu",
  1512	                           weights_only=False)
  1513	        removed_keys = strip_position_id_buffer_keys(state)
  1514	        if len(removed_keys) != 1:
  1515	            fail(5, f"expected exactly one *.embeddings.position_ids buffer "
  1516	                    f"key, found {len(removed_keys)}: {removed_keys}; the "
  1517	                    f"checkpoint is not the understood <=4.30-era artifact")
  1518	        log(f"  r6 enumerated buffer strip: removed {removed_keys[0]!r}")
  1519	        provenance["state_dict_keys_removed_before_load"] = {
  1520	            "pattern": POSITION_ID_BUFFER_PATTERN.pattern,
  1521	            "removed_keys": removed_keys,
  1522	            "reason": ("transformers 4.31.0 made BERT "
  1523	                       "embeddings.position_ids a non-persistent buffer; the "
  1524	                       "<=4.30-era checkpoint carries it (r6 ledger, "
  1525	                       "2026-08-12)"),
  1526	        }
  1527	        write_json(out_dir / "provenance.json", provenance)
  1528	        # strict=True: any OTHER unexpected or missing key must fail here.
  1529	        model.load_state_dict(state, strict=True)
  1530	        model = model.cuda().eval()
  1531	    except SystemExit:
  1532	        raise
  1533	    except (ImportError, ModuleNotFoundError) as e:
  1534	        fail(11, f"released model dependency/import missing: {e!r}")
  1535	    except Exception as e:
  1536	        fail(5, f"released checkpoint/code failed to load unchanged: {e!r}")
  1537	    log("  checkpoint loaded: strict modulo the one enumerated, "
  1538	        "provenance-logged position_ids buffer key (r6)")
  1539	    return model, tokenizer, device

  1569	def dataset_index_for(index_by_name, volume_name):
  1570	    """Map a VolumeName to its released-dataset item, uniquely."""

  1574	        fail(9, f"staged volume {volume_name} not found uniquely in released "
  1575	                f"dataset items: {sorted(index_by_name)}")

  1590	def make_scorer(model, tokenizer, device):
  1591	    """Return score(dataset, idx) -> ([18 float64 probabilities], metrics).
  1592	
  1593	    Empty-string text tokens, exactly as the released inference does; batch
  1594	    size 1 always (contract configuration).
  1595	    """
  1596	    import torch
  1597	    text_tokens = tokenizer([""], return_tensors="pt", padding="max_length",
  1598	                            truncation=True, max_length=512).to("cuda")
  1599	
  1600	    def score(dataset, idx):
  1601	        video, _text, _onehot, _acc = dataset[idx]
  1602	        video = video.unsqueeze(0).cuda()   # batch size 1, per contract
  1603	        torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
  1604	        start = time.monotonic()
  1605	        with torch.no_grad():
  1606	            # Released ImageLatentsClassifier call signature: False asks for
  1607	            # classifier logits rather than the 512-dim latents.
  1608	            logits = model(False, text_tokens, video, device=device)
  1609	        scores = torch.sigmoid(logits).flatten().double().cpu().tolist()
  1610	        return scores, {
  1611	            "seconds": time.monotonic() - start,
  1612	            "peak_gpu_memory_gb": torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1e9,
  1613	        }
  1614	
  1615	    return score

  1815	def chunk_is_complete(chunk_dir):
  1816	    """A chunk counts as complete only when environment.json -- written LAST
  1817	    -- says so and its sibling artifacts exist. Anything else is an
  1818	    interrupted chunk and is redone in full (contract chunk_atomicity)."""
  1819	    env_path = chunk_dir / "environment.json"
  1820	    if not env_path.exists():
  1821	        return False
  1822	    try:
  1823	        env = json.loads(env_path.read_text())
  1824	    except json.JSONDecodeError:
  1825	        return False
  1826	    return (env.get("chunk_complete") is True
  1827	            and (chunk_dir / "chunk_manifest.csv").exists()
  1828	            and (chunk_dir / "per_sample.csv").exists())

  1833	    manifest order (contract determinism.spot_check_subset)."""

  1841	class DownloadLedger:
  1842	    """Persistent download accounting against the R8 volume caps.
  1843	
  1844	    Categories:
  1845	      scientific_first_time -- first chunk-context download of a distinct
  1846	          manifest volume; capped by unique_volume_cap.
  1847	      scientific_shared_reuse -- the same manifest volume downloaded again by
  1848	          a DIFFERENT chunk (volumes shared across strata); reported, not
  1849	          capped (it is neither a distinct volume nor a QA retry).
  1850	      qa_retry -- chunk-redo downloads and anchor (re)downloads; capped by
  1851	          ceil(0.20 x unique_volume_cap) (contract qa_retry_download_allowance).
  1852	    Reaching either cap stops the run as budget exhaustion (exit 9).
  1853	    """
  1854	
  1855	    def __init__(self, path, unique_volume_cap):
  1856	        self.path = path
  1857	        self.unique_cap = unique_volume_cap
  1858	        self.qa_cap = math.ceil(QA_RETRY_FRACTION * unique_volume_cap)
  1859	        rows = read_csv_rows(path)
  1860	        self.first_time_volumes = {
  1861	            r["volume_name"] for r in rows
  1862	            if r["category"] == "scientific_first_time"}
  1863	        self.chunk_seen_volumes = {
  1864	            r["volume_name"] for r in rows
  1865	            if r["context"].startswith("chunk")}
  1866	        self.qa_count = sum(1 for r in rows if r["category"] == "qa_retry")
  1867	
  1868	    def categorize(self, context, volume_name, redo):
  1869	        if context == "anchor" or redo:
  1870	            return "qa_retry"
  1871	        if volume_name in self.chunk_seen_volumes:
  1872	            return "scientific_shared_reuse"
  1873	        return "scientific_first_time"
  1874	
  1875	    def check_budget_before(self, category):
  1876	        """Refuse BEFORE the download that would cross a cap (contract R8:
  1877	        continuing past a cap is invalidating)."""
  1878	        if (category == "scientific_first_time"
  1879	                and len(self.first_time_volumes) + 1 > self.unique_cap):
  1880	            fail(9, f"unique-volume cap {self.unique_cap} would be exceeded; "
  1881	                    f"stopping as budget exhaustion (not a scientific result)")
  1882	        if category == "qa_retry" and self.qa_count + 1 > self.qa_cap:
  1883	            fail(9, f"QA/retry download allowance {self.qa_cap} would be "
  1884	                    f"exceeded; stopping as budget exhaustion "
  1885	                    f"(not a scientific result)")
  1886	
  1887	    def record(self, session_id, context, volume_name, sha256, size_bytes,
  1888	               category):
  1889	        if category == "scientific_first_time":
  1890	            self.first_time_volumes.add(volume_name)
  1891	        if context.startswith("chunk"):
  1892	            self.chunk_seen_volumes.add(volume_name)
  1893	        if category == "qa_retry":
  1894	            self.qa_count += 1
  1895	        append_csv_rows(self.path, [{
  1896	            "timestamp_utc": utc_now(), "session_id": session_id,
  1897	            "context": context, "volume_name": volume_name,
  1898	            "sha256": sha256, "size_bytes": size_bytes,
  1899	            "category": category,
  1900	        }], ACCOUNTING_COLUMNS)
  1901	
  1902	    def totals(self):
  1903	        return {
  1904	            "scientific_first_time": len(self.first_time_volumes),
  1905	            "unique_volume_cap": self.unique_cap,
  1906	            "qa_retry": self.qa_count,
  1907	            "qa_retry_allowance": self.qa_cap,
  1908	        }
  1909	
  1910	
  1911	def run_anchor_protocol(out_dir, work_dir, session_id, tables, scorer,
  1912	                        reference, ledger):
  1913	    """The per-session anchor drift detector (contract execution_model.anchor).
  1914	
  1915	    Runs A, B, A-again on the v1 load-probe pair BEFORE any chunk work.
  1916	    Within-session: the A repeat must be bit-identical (exit 8). Cross-
  1917	    session: per-head probabilities of A and B must sit within 1.0e-4 of the
  1918	    pinned v1 reference (exit 13). All anchor executions are excluded from
  1919	    every scientific statistic and logged to anchor_log.csv.
  1920	    """
  1921	    log("anchor protocol: session drift detector (A, B, A-repeat)")
  1922	    cache_dir = out_dir.parent / "anchor_cache_004"
  1923	    cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1924	    local = {}
  1925	    cached_flags = {}
  1926	    for name in (ANCHOR_VOLUME_A, ANCHOR_VOLUME_B):
  1927	        expected_sha = ANCHOR_INPUT_SHA256[name]
  1928	        cache_path = cache_dir / name
  1929	        # SHA-256 verified before EVERY use, cached or fresh (contract).
  1930	        if cache_path.exists() and sha256_file(cache_path) == expected_sha:
  1931	            cached_flags[name] = True
  1932	        else:
  1933	            ledger.check_budget_before("qa_retry")
  1934	            downloaded = hf_download(volume_repo_path(name),
  1935	                                     work_dir / "anchor_dl")
  1936	            actual = sha256_file(downloaded)
  1937	            if actual != expected_sha:
  1938	                fail(4, f"anchor volume {name} hash {actual} != pinned "
  1939	                        f"{expected_sha}")
  1940	            shutil.copyfile(downloaded, cache_path)
  1941	            ledger.record(session_id, "anchor", name, actual,
  1942	                          cache_path.stat().st_size, "qa_retry")
  1943	            cached_flags[name] = False
  1944	        local[name] = cache_path
  1945	        log(f"  anchor {name}: "
  1946	            f"{'cache hit' if cached_flags[name] else 'downloaded'}, "
  1947	            f"hash verified")
  1948	
  1949	    staging = work_dir / "anchor_stage"
  1950	    if staging.exists():
  1951	        shutil.rmtree(staging)
  1952	    staging.mkdir(parents=True)
  1953	    for name in (ANCHOR_VOLUME_A, ANCHOR_VOLUME_B):
  1954	        stage_volume(local[name], staging, name)
  1955	    dataset, index_by_name = build_inference_dataset(staging, tables)
  1956	    idx_a = dataset_index_for(index_by_name, ANCHOR_VOLUME_A)
  1957	    idx_b = dataset_index_for(index_by_name, ANCHOR_VOLUME_B)
  1958	
  1959	    executions = []
  1960	    for exec_id, idx, name in [("anchor_A", idx_a, ANCHOR_VOLUME_A),
  1961	                               ("anchor_B", idx_b, ANCHOR_VOLUME_B),
  1962	                               ("anchor_A_repeat", idx_a, ANCHOR_VOLUME_A)]:
  1963	        scores, _metrics = scorer(dataset, idx)
  1964	        check_18_finite(scores, exec_id)
  1965	        executions.append((exec_id, name, scores))
  1966	        log(f"  {exec_id}: 18 finite scores")
  1967	
  1968	    a_first = executions[0][2]
  1969	    a_repeat = executions[2][2]
  1970	    bit_identical = scores_hex(a_first) == scores_hex(a_repeat)
  1971	    dev_a = max_abs_deviation(a_first, reference["exec1_A"])
  1972	    dev_b = max_abs_deviation(executions[1][2], reference["exec2_B"])
  1973	
  1974	    rows = []
  1975	    for exec_id, name, scores in executions:
  1976	        dev = {"anchor_A": dev_a, "anchor_B": dev_b}.get(exec_id, "")
  1977	        rows.append({
  1978	            "session_id": session_id,
  1979	            "timestamp_utc": utc_now(),
  1980	            "execution": exec_id,
  1981	            "volume_name": name,
  1982	            "cached": str(cached_flags[name]).lower(),
  1983	            "within_session_bit_identical":
  1984	                str(bit_identical).lower() if exec_id == "anchor_A_repeat"
  1985	                else "",
  1986	            "max_abs_dev_vs_v1": repr(dev) if dev != "" else "",
  1987	            "tolerance": repr(ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE),
  1988	            "in_tolerance":
  1989	                str(bool(dev <= ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE)).lower()
  1990	                if dev != "" else "",
  1991	            "scores_hex": "|".join(scores_hex(scores)),
  1992	        })
  1993	    append_csv_rows(out_dir / "anchor" / "anchor_log.csv", rows,
  1994	                    ANCHOR_LOG_COLUMNS)
  1995	    shutil.rmtree(staging)
  1996	
  1997	    if not bit_identical:
  1998	        fail(8, "anchor A-repeat was NOT bit-identical within this session; "
  1999	                "invalidating determinism failure")
  2000	    if dev_a > ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE or \
  2001	            dev_b > ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE:
  2002	        fail(13, f"anchor cross-session drift beyond the preregistered "
  2003	                 f"{ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE} tolerance "
  2004	                 f"(dev_A={dev_a!r}, dev_B={dev_b!r}); this session's chunks "
  2005	                 f"do not count and are redone after diagnosis")
  2006	    log(f"  anchor in tolerance (dev_A={dev_a:.2e}, dev_B={dev_b:.2e}, "
  2007	        f"tol={ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE}); A-repeat bit-identical")
  2008

  2581	        fail(12, f"smoke self-test failed: {what}; harness fault")
