  2584	def smoke_write_chunk(out_dir, session_id, chunk_no, chunk_pairs, spot_ids,
  2585	                      complete=True):
  2586	    """Write chunk artifacts exactly the way run_chunk does, with mock
  2587	    downloads and scores. complete=False simulates an interruption after the
  2588	    chunk manifest (so completeness detection and redo can be tested)."""
  2589	    chunk_dir = chunk_dir_for(out_dir, chunk_no)
  2590	    if chunk_dir.exists():
  2591	        shutil.rmtree(chunk_dir)
  2592	    chunk_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
  2593	    manifest_rows = []
  2594	    for pair in chunk_pairs:
  2595	        for role in ("softer", "sharper"):
  2596	            name = pair[f"volume_name_{role}"]
  2597	            fake_bytes = ("synthetic-volume:" + name).encode()
  2598	            manifest_rows.append({
  2599	                "chunk": f"chunk_{chunk_no:02d}", "pair_id": pair["pair_id"],
  2600	                "role": role, "volume_name": name,
  2601	                "sha256": sha256_bytes(fake_bytes),
  2602	                "size_bytes": len(fake_bytes),
  2603	                "session_id": session_id, "downloaded_utc": utc_now(),
  2604	            })
  2605	    write_csv(chunk_dir / "chunk_manifest.csv", manifest_rows,
  2606	              CHUNK_MANIFEST_COLUMNS)
  2607	    if not complete:
  2608	        return  # simulated interruption: no per_sample, no environment.json
  2609	    sample_rows = []
  2610	    for pair in chunk_pairs:
  2611	        for role in ("softer", "sharper"):
  2612	            name = pair[f"volume_name_{role}"]
  2613	            scores = smoke_mock_scores(name)
  2614	            hexes = scores_hex(scores)
  2615	            for h in range(18):
  2616	                sample_rows.append({
  2617	                    "session_id": session_id,
  2618	                    "chunk": f"chunk_{chunk_no:02d}",
  2619	                    "execution": f"{pair['pair_id']}_{role}",
  2620	                    "pair_id": pair["pair_id"], "role": role,
  2621	                    "volume_name": name, "stratum": pair["stratum"],
  2622	                    "head_index": h, "head_name": EXPECTED_PATHOLOGIES[h],
  2623	                    "score": repr(float(scores[h])), "score_hex": hexes[h],
  2624	                })
  2625	        if pair["pair_id"] in spot_ids:
  2626	            # Spot-check logic: a deterministic re-run must be bit-identical.
  2627	            for role in ("softer", "sharper"):
  2628	                name = pair[f"volume_name_{role}"]
  2629	                rerun_hex = scores_hex(smoke_mock_scores(name))
  2630	                first_hex = scores_hex(smoke_mock_scores(name))
  2631	                smoke_assert(rerun_hex == first_hex,
  2632	                             f"spot-check re-run not bit-identical for "
  2633	                             f"{pair['pair_id']}/{role}")
  2634	    write_csv(chunk_dir / "per_sample.csv", sample_rows,
  2635	              CHUNK_PER_SAMPLE_COLUMNS)
  2636	    write_json(chunk_dir / "environment.json", {
  2637	        "chunk": f"chunk_{chunk_no:02d}", "chunk_complete": True,
  2638	        "session_id": session_id, "mock": True,
  2639	        "timestamp_utc": utc_now(),
  2640	    })
  2641	
  2642	
  2643	def run_smoke(out_dir, gate_info):
  2644	    log("SMOKE: synthetic harness test (cannot satisfy the contract)")
  2645	    capture_environment(out_dir, "SMOKE", need_gpu=False)
  2646	    write_resolved_config(out_dir, "SMOKE", extra={
  2647	        "smoke_overrides": {
  2648	            "stratum_counts": SMOKE_STRATUM_COUNTS,
  2649	            "chunk_size": SMOKE_CHUNK_SIZE,
  2650	            "replicates": SMOKE_REPLICATES,
  2651	            "sparse_min": SMOKE_SPARSE_MIN,
  2652	        }})
  2653	    write_json(out_dir / "provenance.json", {
  2654	        "mode": "smoke",
  2655	        "note": ("synthetic run; no external artifact was touched; the real "
  2656	                 "hash-bound approval gate DID run against the committed "
  2657	                 "contract and marker"),
  2658	        "contract_blob": gate_info["contract_blob"],
  2659	    })
  2660	    checks = {}
  2661	
  2662	    # -- 1. pure-function self-tests -------------------------------------
  2663	    log("SMOKE 1: pure-function self-tests")
  2664	    # git blob function against the canonical known vector: the git blob of
  2665	    # b"hello\n" is ce013625030ba8dba906f756967f9e9ca394464a.
  2666	    smoke_assert(git_blob_sha1_bytes(b"hello\n")
  2667	                 == "ce013625030ba8dba906f756967f9e9ca394464a",
  2668	                 "git_blob_sha1_bytes known vector")
  2669	    checks["git_blob_function_known_vector"] = True
  2670	    # The gate already verified marker blob == contract blob (real files).
  2671	    checks["hash_bound_gate_verified_real_contract"] = True
  2672	
  2673	    for raw, want in [
  2674	        ("['Br40f', '3']", "Br40f"),      # frozen release: stringified list
  2675	        ("  Br60f ", "Br60f"),             # plain string
  2676	        ("['Bl56f']", "Bl56f"),            # one-element list literal
  2677	        ("[not a literal", "[not a literal"),  # unparsable -> stripped raw
  2678	    ]:
  2679	        smoke_assert(normalize_kernel(raw) == want,
  2680	                     f"normalize_kernel({raw!r})")
  2681	    checks["kernel_normalization_both_formats"] = True
  2682	
  2683	    observed_key = "trained_model.text_transformer.embeddings.position_ids"
  2684	    near_misses = {
  2685	        "embeddings.position_ids": 0,   # no leading component
  2686	        "trained_model.text_transformer.embeddings."
  2687	        "position_embeddings.weight": 1,   # learnable weights, not the buffer
  2688	        "a.embeddings.position_ids_extra": 2,   # suffix must be anchored
  2689	    }
  2690	    state = dict.fromkeys([observed_key, *near_misses], 0)
  2691	    smoke_assert(strip_position_id_buffer_keys(state) == [observed_key],
  2692	                 "buffer strip removes exactly the observed key")
  2693	    smoke_assert(set(state) == set(near_misses),
  2694	                 "buffer strip leaves near-miss keys untouched")
  2695	    smoke_assert(strip_position_id_buffer_keys(dict(near_misses)) == [],
  2696	                 "buffer strip matches no near-miss key")
  2697	    two = {observed_key: 0, "other.encoder.embeddings.position_ids": 1}
  2698	    smoke_assert(len(strip_position_id_buffer_keys(two)) == 2,
  2699	                 "buffer strip reports both keys in the two-key case")
  2700	    checks["r6_buffer_strip_enumerated_and_anchored"] = True
  2701	
  2702	    smoke_assert(kernel_numeric_index("Br40f") == 40
  2703	                 and kernel_numeric_index("Bl57d") == 57,
  2704	                 "kernel numeric index")
  2705	    assert_sign_directions()   # numeric R2 rule == contract's explicit table
  2706	    checks["sign_directions_match_contract_table"] = True
  2707	
  2708	    # Phase-B entry refusals (pure). The placeholder branch is tested on a
  2709	    # SYNTHETIC text so smoke keeps passing after the real contract is
  2710	    # amended; the real contract is then checked against whichever state it
  2711	    # is legitimately in (frozen pre-amendment, or amended).
  2712	    contract_text = CONTRACT_PATH.read_text()
  2713	    blob = gate_info["contract_blob"]
  2714	    frozen_text = f'still frozen: "{PLACEHOLDER}"\n'
  2715	    ok, reason, _, _ = phase_b_entry_check(frozen_text, "X" * 40, "X" * 40)
  2716	    smoke_assert(not ok and "placeholder" in reason,
  2717	                 "phase B refused while placeholders remain")
  2718	    checks["phase_b_refused_placeholder"] = True
  2719	    ok, reason, _, _ = phase_b_entry_check(contract_text, "0" * 40, blob)
  2720	    smoke_assert(not ok and "blob" in reason,
  2721	                 "phase B refused on a stale marker blob")
  2722	    checks["phase_b_refused_stale_marker"] = True
  2723	    ok, reason, _, _ = phase_b_entry_check(contract_text, blob, blob)
  2724	    if PLACEHOLDER in contract_text:
  2725	        smoke_assert(not ok and "placeholder" in reason,
  2726	                     "pre-amendment real contract refuses phase B")
  2727	        checks["real_contract_phase_b_state"] = "refused_pre_amendment"
  2728	    else:
  2729	        smoke_assert(ok, "amended real contract passes the entry check")
  2730	        checks["real_contract_phase_b_state"] = "amended_entry_ok"
  2731	    amended = ('pair_manifest_sha256: "' + "a" * 64 + '"\n'
  2732	               'unique_volume_count: 15\n')
  2733	    ok, _, sha, count = phase_b_entry_check(amended, "X" * 40, "X" * 40)
  2734	    smoke_assert(ok and sha == "a" * 64 and count == 15,
  2735	                 "phase B accepts a properly amended contract")
  2736	    checks["phase_b_accepts_amended"] = True
  2737	
  2738	    value, clipped = logit_clipped(0.5)
  2739	    smoke_assert(value == 0.0 and not clipped, "logit at 0.5")
  2740	    value, clipped = logit_clipped(0.0)
  2741	    smoke_assert(clipped and value < -13, "logit clip at 0.0")
  2742	    checks["logit_clip_counting"] = True
  2743	
  2744	    smoke_assert(max_abs_deviation([0.5] * 18,
  2745	                                   [0.5 + 5e-5] + [0.5] * 17)
  2746	                 <= ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE,
  2747	                 "anchor tolerance passes a 5e-5 deviation")
  2748	    smoke_assert(max_abs_deviation([0.5] * 18,
  2749	                                   [0.5 + 2e-4] + [0.5] * 17)
  2750	                 > ANCHOR_CROSS_SESSION_TOLERANCE,
  2751	                 "anchor tolerance flags a 2e-4 deviation")
  2752	    checks["anchor_tolerance_logic"] = True
  2753	
  2754	    # -- 2. synthetic phase M: manifest generation with planted decoys ----
  2755	    log("SMOKE 2: four-stratum manifest generation with planted decoys")
  2756	    rows = smoke_metadata_rows()
  2757	    pairs, audit = build_pair_manifest(
  2758	        rows, SMOKE_STRATUM_COUNTS, (ANCHOR_VOLUME_A, ANCHOR_VOLUME_B))
  2759	    manifest_dir = out_dir / "manifest"
  2760	    manifest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  2761	    write_json(manifest_dir / "selection_audit.json", audit)
  2762	    smoke_assert(audit["counts_match"],
  2763	                 f"stratum counts {audit['selected_counts']} != planted "
  2764	                 f"{SMOKE_STRATUM_COUNTS}")
  2765	    smoke_assert(audit["non_validation_refused"] == 3,
  2766	                 f"split guard refused {audit['non_validation_refused']} "
  2767	                 f"rows, expected 3 (2 train + 1 test)")
  2768	    s1 = audit["per_stratum"]["Br40f|Br60f"]
  2769	    smoke_assert(s1["candidate_pairs_failing_geometry_match"] == 1
  2770	                 and s1["geometry_mismatches_by_column"] == {"ZSpacing": 1},
  2771	                 "geometry decoy dropped for exactly ZSpacing")
  2772	    smoke_assert(s1["scans_with_duplicate_contrast_members"] == 1,
  2773	                 "duplicate-member decoy dropped")
  2774	    smoke_assert(audit["unique_volume_count"] == 17,
  2775	                 f"unique volumes {audit['unique_volume_count']} != 17 "
  2776	                 f"(one shared volume across strata)")
  2777	    checks["manifest_counts_and_decoys"] = True
  2778	    checks["unique_volume_count_not_2x_pairs"] = True
  2779	
  2780	    smoke_assert(pairs[0]["pair_id"] == "p001"
  2781	                 and pairs[0]["anchor_excluded"] == "true"
  2782	                 and pairs[0]["stratum"] == "Br40f|Br60f",
  2783	                 "anchor pair flagged at p001")
  2784	    smoke_assert(all(p["anchor_excluded"] == "false" for p in pairs[1:]),
  2785	                 "exactly one anchor pair")
  2786	    checks["anchor_pair_flagged"] = True
  2787	
  2788	    manifest_bytes = manifest_csv_bytes(pairs)
  2789	    smoke_assert(manifest_bytes == manifest_csv_bytes(pairs),
  2790	                 "manifest serialization deterministic")
  2791	    (manifest_dir / "pair_manifest.csv").write_bytes(manifest_bytes)
  2792	    log(f"  smoke manifest: {len(pairs)} pairs, sha256 "
  2793	        f"{sha256_bytes(manifest_bytes)[:12]}...")
  2794	    checks["manifest_deterministic_bytes"] = True
  2795	
  2796	    # softer/sharper assignment sanity on the cross-family stratum: Br40f
  2797	    # (index 40) must be the softer member against Bl56f (index 56).
  2798	    p_cross = [p for p in pairs if p["stratum"] == "Bl56f|Br40f"][0]
  2799	    smoke_assert(p_cross["kernel_normalized_softer"] == "Br40f"
  2800	                 and p_cross["kernel_normalized_sharper"] == "Bl56f",
  2801	                 "cross-family softer/sharper assignment")
  2802	
  2803	    # -- 3. synthetic phase B: chunks, interruption, resume, collection ---
  2804	    log("SMOKE 3: chunk cycle with a simulated interruption and redo")
  2805	    chunks = chunk_plan(pairs, SMOKE_CHUNK_SIZE)
  2806	    smoke_assert([len(c) for _, c in chunks] == [4, 4, 1],
  2807	                 "chunk plan 4+4+1 over 9 pairs")
  2808	    spot_ids = spot_check_pair_ids(pairs)
  2809	    smoke_assert(spot_ids == {"p001", "p006", "p008", "p009"},
  2810	                 f"spot-check subset {sorted(spot_ids)}")
  2811	    session1 = "smoke-session-1"
  2812	    smoke_write_chunk(out_dir, session1, 1, chunks[0][1], spot_ids)
  2813	    smoke_write_chunk(out_dir, session1, 2, chunks[1][1], spot_ids)
  2814	    smoke_write_chunk(out_dir, session1, 3, chunks[2][1], spot_ids,
  2815	                      complete=False)   # simulated interruption
  2816	    states = [chunk_is_complete(chunk_dir_for(out_dir, n)) for n, _ in chunks]
  2817	    smoke_assert(states == [True, True, False],
  2818	                 f"completeness detection saw {states}")
  2819	    checks["interrupted_chunk_detected"] = True
  2820	    # "Session 2" redoes the interrupted chunk in full.
  2821	    smoke_write_chunk(out_dir, "smoke-session-2", 3, chunks[2][1], spot_ids)
  2822	    smoke_assert(chunk_is_complete(chunk_dir_for(out_dir, 3)),
  2823	                 "redone chunk complete")
  2824	    checks["interrupted_chunk_redone_in_full"] = True
  2825	    checks["spot_check_bit_identity_logic"] = True
  2826	
  2827	    scores_by_pair, scientific_rows = collect_chunk_scores(out_dir, pairs,
  2828	                                                           chunks)
  2829	    smoke_assert(len(scientific_rows) == len(pairs) * 2 * 18,
  2830	                 f"{len(scientific_rows)} scientific rows != "
  2831	                 f"{len(pairs) * 2 * 18}")
  2832	    # Round-trip exactness: collected floats equal the mock scorer's output.
  2833	    smoke_assert(scores_by_pair["p002"]["softer"]
  2834	                 == smoke_mock_scores("valid_10_a_1.nii.gz"),
  2835	                 "score_hex round-trip exactness")
  2836	    checks["chunk_score_collection_roundtrip"] = True
  2837	
  2838	    # Bit-identity must FAIL for different inputs (negative control).
  2839	    smoke_assert(scores_hex(smoke_mock_scores("valid_10_a_1.nii.gz"))
  2840	                 != scores_hex(smoke_mock_scores("valid_10_a_2.nii.gz")),
  2841	                 "different volumes give different score bytes")
  2842	
  2843	    # -- 4. analysis on mock scores (numpy/sklearn permitting) ------------
  2844	    analysis_status = "full"
  2845	    try:
  2846	        import numpy  # noqa: F401  (availability probe only)
  2847	    except ImportError:
  2848	        analysis_status = "skipped_no_numpy"
  2849	    if analysis_status == "full":
  2850	        log("SMOKE 4: tier-1 analysis on mock scores")
  2851	        diff_rows, stat_rows, boot_rows = tier1_compute(
  2852	            pairs, scores_by_pair, SMOKE_REPLICATES, TIER1_BOOTSTRAP_SEED,
  2853	            CONFIRMATORY_STRATA, EXPLORATORY_STRATA)
  2854	        smoke_assert(len(diff_rows) == len(pairs) * 18,
  2855	                     f"{len(diff_rows)} tier-1 difference rows")
  2856	        smoke_assert(len(stat_rows) == 3 * 18 * 2,
  2857	                     f"{len(stat_rows)} tier-1 stat cells (3 confirmatory "
  2858	                     f"strata x 18 heads x 2 scales)")
  2859	        smoke_assert(len(boot_rows) == 3 * 18 * 2 * 3,
  2860	                     f"{len(boot_rows)} tier-1 bootstrap rows")
  2861	        smoke_assert(not any(r["stratum"] == "Br40f|Br44f"
  2862	                             for r in stat_rows),
  2863	                     "no summary statistics for the exploratory stratum")
  2864	        s1_rows = [r for r in stat_rows if r["stratum"] == "Br40f|Br60f"]
  2865	        smoke_assert(all(r["n_raw"] == 5 and r["n_counted"] == 4
  2866	                         for r in s1_rows),
  2867	                     "anchor pair excluded from counted N (5 raw, 4 counted)")
  2868	        # Sign check on one concrete cell: d = sharper - softer, exactly.
  2869	        want = (smoke_mock_scores("valid_10_a_2.nii.gz")[0]
  2870	                - smoke_mock_scores("valid_10_a_1.nii.gz")[0])
  2871	        got = [float(r["d_prob"]) for r in diff_rows
  2872	               if r["pair_id"] == "p002" and r["head_index"] == 0]
  2873	        smoke_assert(got == [want], "signed difference is sharper - softer")
  2874	        smoke_assert(all(r["ci95_lo"] <= r["ci95_hi"] for r in boot_rows),
  2875	                     "bootstrap interval ordering")
  2876	        write_csv(out_dir / "analysis" / "tier1_differences.csv", diff_rows,
  2877	                  ["pair_id", "patient_id", "stratum", "anchor_excluded",
  2878	                   "exploratory", "head_index", "head_name", "softer_prob",
  2879	                   "sharper_prob", "d_prob", "d_logit", "n_clipped_members"])
  2880	        write_csv(out_dir / "analysis" / "tier1_stats.csv", stat_rows,
  2881	                  ["stratum", "head_index", "head_name", "scale", "n_raw",
  2882	                   "n_counted", "median_signed", "mean_signed", "q50_abs",
  2883	                   "q75_abs", "q90_abs", "q95_abs", "max_abs",
  2884	                   "n_clipped_members"])
  2885	        write_csv(out_dir / "analysis" / "tier1_bootstrap.csv", boot_rows,
  2886	                  ["stratum", "head_index", "head_name", "scale",
  2887	                   "statistic", "point", "ci95_lo", "ci95_hi", "replicates",
  2888	                   "n_patients", "n_counted_pairs"])
  2889	        checks["tier1_rows_and_exclusions"] = True
  2890	
  2891	        try:
  2892	            import sklearn  # noqa: F401
  2893	            have_sklearn = True
  2894	        except ImportError:
  2895	            have_sklearn = False
  2896	            analysis_status = "tier2_skipped_no_sklearn"
  2897	        if have_sklearn:
  2898	            log("SMOKE 4: tier-2 analysis on mock scores and planted labels")
  2899	            # Planted labels: head 0 has 2 positive and 2 negative counted
  2900	            # stratum-1 pairs (eligible at the smoke minimum of 2); every
  2901	            # other head is all-negative (excluded). Both members of a pair
  2902	            # always carry the same row, matching the Stage-0 premise.
  2903	            labels_by_volume = {}
  2904	            for pair in pairs:
  2905	                for role in ("softer", "sharper"):
  2906	                    name = pair[f"volume_name_{role}"]
  2907	                    head0 = 1 if pair["patient_id"] in ("valid_10",
  2908	                                                        "valid_1") else 0
  2909	                    labels_by_volume[name] = [head0] + [0] * 17
  2910	            smoke_assert(label_integrity_problems(pairs, labels_by_volume)
  2911	                         == [], "planted labels pass integrity")
  2912	            doctored = dict(labels_by_volume)
  2913	            doctored["valid_10_a_2.nii.gz"] = [0] + [0] * 17
  2914	            smoke_assert(len(label_integrity_problems(pairs, doctored)) == 1,
  2915	                         "label integrity detects a planted mismatch")
  2916	            checks["label_integrity_detects_planted_mismatch"] = True
  2917	
  2918	            auroc_rows, excluded_rows = tier2_compute(
  2919	                pairs, scores_by_pair, labels_by_volume, SMOKE_REPLICATES,
  2920	                TIER2_BOOTSTRAP_SEED, SMOKE_SPARSE_MIN, SMOKE_SPARSE_MIN)
  2921	            smoke_assert(len(auroc_rows) == 1
  2922	                         and auroc_rows[0]["stratum"] == "Br40f|Br60f"
  2923	                         and auroc_rows[0]["head_index"] == 0,
  2924	                         f"exactly one eligible tier-2 cell, got "
  2925	                         f"{[(r['stratum'], r['head_index']) for r in auroc_rows]}")
  2926	            smoke_assert(len(excluded_rows) == 4 * 18 - 1,
  2927	                         f"{len(excluded_rows)} excluded cells != 71")
  2928	            smoke_assert(all(r["reason"] == EXCLUDED_CELL_LABEL
  2929	                             for r in excluded_rows),
  2930	                         "excluded cells carry the mandated label")
  2931	            smoke_assert(0.0 <= auroc_rows[0]["auroc_sharper"] <= 1.0
  2932	                         and 0.0 <= auroc_rows[0]["auroc_softer"] <= 1.0,
  2933	                         "AUROC in [0, 1]")
  2934	            write_csv(out_dir / "analysis" / "tier2_auroc.csv", auroc_rows,
  2935	                      ["stratum", "head_index", "head_name",
  2936	                       "n_counted_pairs", "n_positive", "n_negative",
  2937	                       "auroc_sharper", "auroc_softer", "delta_auroc",
  2938	                       "auroc_sharper_ci95_lo", "auroc_sharper_ci95_hi",
  2939	                       "auroc_softer_ci95_lo", "auroc_softer_ci95_hi",
  2940	                       "delta_auroc_ci95_lo", "delta_auroc_ci95_hi",
  2941	                       "replicates", "nan_replicates"])
  2942	            write_csv(out_dir / "analysis" / "tier2_excluded_cells.csv",
  2943	                      excluded_rows,
  2944	                      ["stratum", "head_index", "head_name",
  2945	                       "n_counted_pairs", "n_positive", "n_negative",
  2946	                       "reason"])
  2947	            checks["tier2_sparse_rule_both_branches"] = True
  2948	    else:
  2949	        log("SMOKE 4: numpy not importable; analysis portion SKIPPED "
  2950	            "(install probes/004/requirements.txt and re-run smoke for "
  2951	            "full coverage before any real phase-B run)")
  2952	
  2953	    # -- 5. summary -------------------------------------------------------
  2954	    log("SMOKE 5: summary")
  2955	    summary = {
  2956	        "idea_id": IDEA_ID,
  2957	        "contract_version": CONTRACT_VERSION,
  2958	        "phase": "SMOKE",
  2959	        "mode": "smoke",
  2960	        "contract_satisfied": False,   # a smoke run can NEVER satisfy it
  2961	        "analysis_coverage": analysis_status,
  2962	        "harness_checks": checks,
  2963	        "interpretation": (
  2964	            "SMOKE MODE ONLY. This run used synthetic metadata, mock scores, "
  2965	            "and planted decoys to verify the harness: the hash-bound "
  2966	            "approval gate (against the real committed contract and marker), "
  2967	            "the phase-B entry refusals, four-stratum manifest generation "
  2968	            "and its hard gate, deterministic serialization, chunk "
  2969	            "completeness/redo detection, spot-check and anchor "
  2970	            "bit-identity/tolerance logic, and the frozen tier-1/tier-2 "
  2971	            "analysis code. It says NOTHING about the contract's risky "
  2972	            "assumption -- whether the provenance-frozen pipeline scales to "
  2973	            "425 real pairs -- and no number produced here is evidence "
  2974	            "about reconstruction sensitivity. Only the real phase M and "
  2975	            "phase B runs, in that order and each under its own approval, "
  2976	            "can produce the contract's positive_pattern."),
  2977	    }
  2978	    write_json(out_dir / "summary.json", summary)
  2979	    print()
  2980	    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
  2981	    print()
  2982	    print("Plain-English read: the harness plumbing works end to end"
  2983	          + (" (analysis included)." if analysis_status == "full" else
  2984	             f" EXCEPT: {analysis_status}."))
  2985	    print("The scientific pipeline remains untested until the real runs.")
  2986	    return 0
  2987	
  2988	
  2989	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2990	# ENTRY POINT.

codex
I found one contract-level resume defect: the 30-session cap counts only sessions that reach and write the anchor log. Failed Phase B sessions before that point—access, checkpoint load, environment, or early anchor failures—can be retried indefinitely without consuming the cap. Because R8 caps sessions, not successful anchors, this is blocking even though the scientific analysis logic is otherwise faithful.
apply patch
patch: completed

+## Blocking finding
 
-## Contract fidelity
+### 1. The session cap counts successful anchor-log sessions, not Phase B sessions
 
-- **The authorized compatibility exception is exact and fail-closed.** The regex
-  is suffix-anchored and requires a leading component
-  (`probes/004/run.py:626-643`). The real load requires exactly one matching key;
-  zero or multiple matches exit 5, and `strict=True` still rejects every other
-  missing or unexpected key (`probes/004/run.py:1174-1203`). This matches the r6
-  ledger's updated meaning of “unchanged modulo enumerated, provenance-logged
-  framework-era buffer keys.”
-- **The exception is auditable.** The removed key, pattern, and reason are written
-  to `provenance.json` before strict loading and printed to the run log
-  (`probes/004/run.py:1186-1199`). The installed Transformers version is captured
-  in `environment.txt` and logged at startup (`probes/004/run.py:335-353`).
-- **No experiment-scope drift.** Pair selection, the A/B/A execution sequence,
-  one-seed limit, 45-minute cap, 18-output checks, bit-determinism check, and
-  required outputs remain intact. The dependency closure is restored to
-  `transformers==4.38.2` and `tokenizers==0.15.2` as directed
-  (`probes/004/requirements.txt:8-16`).
+Contract R8 freezes a cap of 30 **sessions**. The driver instead reconstructs
+usage solely from distinct `session_id` values in `anchor/anchor_log.csv`
+(`probes/004/run.py:2321-2329`). A new session is not durably registered at
+entry. The anchor log is written only after the model and both anchor inputs
+have loaded and all three anchor executions have completed
+(`probes/004/run.py:1959-1995`).
 
-## Silent-failure surfaces
+Consequently, Phase B sessions that fail during environment capture, metadata
+or checkpoint access, checkpoint loading, anchor preprocessing, or anchor
+inference do not consume the session cap. The operator can retry those paths
+indefinitely while the code continues to report fewer than 30 sessions. This
+violates `budgets.session_cap: 30` and the stopping rule requiring the run to
+stop when any R8 cap would be exceeded. It is also a silent accounting error:
+`summary.json` reports `sessions_used` from the same incomplete anchor-derived
+count (`probes/004/run.py:2443-2445`).
 
-No blocking silent-failure surface was found. The buffer strip cannot silently
-generalize to arbitrary checkpoint differences: near-miss names remain untouched,
-and strict loading follows immediately. Smoke mode explicitly tests the observed
-key, near misses, and zero/two-match behavior (`probes/004/run.py:802-832`). Any
-other load problem remains an exit-5 failure rather than a successful result.
+Required repair: persist a Phase B session-attempt record before any access,
+model, or anchor work, refuse the 31st attempt before it begins, and derive
+both enforcement and `sessions_used` from that durable record. Do not expand
+the experiment or alter any scientific endpoint.
